<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0001 -->
# 四级单词 0001 · 调查；探究
中文：调查；探究
单词：###inquiry###
搭配：###conduct an inquiry### 开展调查
例句：Amongst the staff, the ###inquiry### produced different interpretations, but basically everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0002 -->

中文：解释；理解
单词：###interpretation###
搭配：###different interpretations### 不同的解释
例句：Amongst the staff, the inquiry produced different ###interpretations###, but basically everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0003 -->
# 四级单词 0003 · 一致；符合
中文：一致；符合
单词：###accordance###
搭配：###in accordance with### 根据；与……一致
例句：Amongst the staff, the inquiry produced different interpretations, but basically everyone acted in ###accordance### with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w0004 -->

单词：###basically###
搭配：###basically the same### 基本相同
例句：Amongst the staff, the inquiry produced different interpretations, but ###basically### everyone acted in accordance with the final decision.
译文：在员工当中，这项调查产生了不同的理解，但基本上每个人都按照最终决定行事。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###amongst###

# 四级单词 0006 · 顺序
中文：顺序
单词：###sequence###
搭配：###in the correct sequence### 
例句：When you skim the report, specify the exact ###sequence### of the specimen tests; other details are secondary.
译文：当你略读报告时，请明确说明标本测试的具体顺序；其他细节则是次要的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###classical music### 古典音乐
例句：Whatever critics say, this masterpiece will remain a classic admired by generations of readers and scholars of ###classical### literature.
译文：无论评论家怎么说，这部杰作都将是一部经典，受到一代代读者和古典文学学者的推崇。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 四级单词 0795 · 痛苦的
中文：痛苦的
单词：###painful###
搭配：###a painful experience### 痛苦的经历
例句：Failure is undoubtedly ###painful###, yet it often offers lessons more valuable than success, so we should not feel hopeless or miserable.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1073 -->
# 四级单词 1073 · 应受（罚）的；易于……的
中文：应受（罚）的；易于……的
单词：###liable###
搭配：###be liable to a fine### 应被罚款
例句：All baggage must be checked and its straps fastened; anyone carrying an ax on board is ###liable### to a fine.
译文：所有行李都必须检查并扣好绑带；携带斧头登机的人会被罚款。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1074 -->

译文：我们续签租约并粉刷了两层小楼的墙壁时，把那些没用的家具扔了出去。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Did you hear ###scratching### above the ceiling? I hope it's not a rat! — Relax, it's the owl on the roof, or maybe a crow.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Mum, a bee stung me while I was picking limes! — Stop ###sobbing### and moaning, and don't suck the spot; I'll put some ice on it.

译文：妈妈，我摘酸橙的时候被蜜蜂蜇了！——别哭哭啼啼、哼哼唧唧了，也别去吸伤口，我给你敷点冰。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1298 -->
# 四级单词 1298 · 酸橙；石灰
中文：酸橙；石灰
单词：###lime###
搭配：###lime juice### 酸橙汁
例句：Mum, a bee stung me while I was picking ###limes###! — Stop sobbing and moaning, and don't suck the spot; I'll put some ice on it.
译文：妈妈，我摘酸橙的时候被蜜蜂蜇了！——别哭哭啼啼、哼哼唧唧了，也别去吸伤口，我给你敷点冰。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1299 -->

搭配：###assemble a computer### 组装电脑
例句：Have you finished ###assembling### the bookshelf? — The wooden framework is done, but I can't fit the metal clasps, and the instructions are confusing.
译文：书架装好了吗？——木框架装好了，可金属扣件装不上，说明书也看得人糊涂。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a music lover### 音乐爱好者
例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car ###lover###! Which manufacturer? — A local one. — Insure it; you can't possibly drive without cover.
译文：我刚买了第一辆车！——你这个车迷！哪个厂家的？——本地的。——去上保险吧，没保险可不能开。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1324 -->

例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car lover! Which ###manufacturer###? — A local one. — Insure it; you can't possibly drive without cover.

搭配：###cannot possibly### 绝不可能
例句：I've just bought my first car! — You car lover! Which manufacturer? — A local one. — Insure it; you can't ###possibly### drive without cover.
译文：我刚买了第一辆车！——你这个车迷！哪个厂家的？——本地的。——去上保险吧，没保险可不能开。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1327 -->
# 四级单词 1327 · 使明亮
中文：使明亮
单词：###lighten###
搭配：###lighten the room### 使房间明亮
例句：What lovely roses! They ###lighten### the whole room. — Thanks. They're still wet with dew; cut the stems and they'll bloom for days.
译文：多漂亮的玫瑰！把屋子都照亮了。——谢谢。它们还带着露水，把花茎剪一下，能开好几天。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1328 -->

例句：This machine keeps jamming! — The paper is partly stuck. Check the roller, and feed sheets of different thickness ###separately###.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1335 -->
# 四级单词 1335 · 某个时候
中文：某个时候
单词：###sometime###
搭配：###sometime next week### 下周某个时候
例句：Let's meet ###sometime### next week. — Sure. Friday is probable, but it isn't definite yet; dial my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1336 -->

搭配：###a probable result### 可能的结果
例句：Let's meet sometime next week. — Sure. Friday is ###probable###, but it isn't definite yet; dial my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1337 -->

搭配：###a definite answer### 明确的答复
例句：Let's meet sometime next week. — Sure. Friday is probable, but it isn't ###definite### yet; dial my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1338 -->
# 四级单词 1338 · 拨（号）
中文：拨（号）
单词：###dial###
搭配：###dial a number### 拨号码
例句：Let's meet sometime next week. — Sure. Friday is probable, but it isn't definite yet; ###dial### my office tomorrow.
译文：下周找个时间见个面吧。——好啊。很可能是周五，但还没定下来，明天打我办公室电话吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1339 -->
# 四级单词 1339 · 不同意
中文：不同意
单词：###disagree###
搭配：###disagree with sb.### 不同意某人
例句：I ###disagree### with the view that failure is purely harmful; with few exceptions, it offers valuable, if indirect, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1340 -->

例句：I disagree with the view that failure is ###purely### harmful; with few exceptions, it offers valuable, if indirect, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1341 -->
# 四级单词 1341 · 例外
中文：例外
单词：###exception###
搭配：###without exception### 无一例外
例句：I disagree with the view that failure is purely harmful; with few ###exceptions###, it offers valuable, if indirect, lessons.
译文：我不同意失败只有坏处这个观点；除了少数例外，它能带来宝贵的教训，哪怕是间接的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###Thirdly, ...### 第三，……
例句：I'm so tense before the exam! — Relax. First, read carefully; secondly, underline key words; ###thirdly###, check your answers.
译文：考试前我好紧张！——放松。第一，仔细读题；第二，划出关键词；第三，检查答案。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1508 -->

搭配：###a feeling of sadness### 悲伤的感觉

译文：怎么这么难过？——我又没考过。——别难过了！这个能让你振作起来、开心一整天：音乐会门票！激动吗？
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1517 -->

搭配：###on the spur of the moment### 一时冲动
例句：Why did you start jogging? — I used to breathe heavily on the stairs; that was the ###spur###, and determination helps me persist.
译文：你为什么开始慢跑？——我以前爬楼梯喘得厉害，这就是动力，决心让我坚持了下来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1520 -->
# 四级单词 1520 · 有男子气概的
中文：有男子气概的
单词：###manly###
搭配：###a manly voice### 浑厚的嗓音
例句：Grandpa looks so ###manly### shearing sheep! — Can I help? — Sure. Harness the horse, then pitch the wool onto the waggon.
译文：爷爷剪羊毛的样子真有男子气概！——我能帮忙吗？——行。先给马套上挽具，再把羊毛扔到车上。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1524 -->
# 四级单词 1524 · 图表

单词：###graph###
搭配：###a line graph### 折线图

译文：这个应用怎么这么慢？——仔细看这张图：用户数已经连续三天猛增了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1525 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1528 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1532 -->
# 四级单词 1532 · 滑翔
中文：滑翔
单词：###glide###
搭配：###glide through the air### 在空中滑翔
例句：Look at that eagle ###gliding### with wings spread outward! — Now it's diving downward and thrusting out its claws.
译文：看那只老鹰展开翅膀在滑翔！——它现在俯冲下来，伸出了爪子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1533 -->

搭配：###spread outward### 向外扩展

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1535 -->

搭配：###thrust sth. into ...### 把某物插进……
例句：Look at that eagle gliding with wings spread outward! — Now it's diving downward and ###thrusting### out its claws.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1536 -->

中文：倒影；反思
单词：###reflection###
搭配：###a reflection in the water### 水中的倒影
例句：Look at the ###reflection### of the peaks in the lake! — It resembles a mirror, only reversed. The place feels almost holy.
译文：看湖中山峰的倒影！——像一面镜子，只是倒过来的。这地方简直有种神圣感。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1537 -->

搭配：###resemble one's father### 长得像父亲
例句：Look at the reflection of the peaks in the lake! — It ###resembles### a mirror, only reversed. The place feels almost holy.
译文：看湖中山峰的倒影！——像一面镜子，只是倒过来的。这地方简直有种神圣感。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a holy place### 圣地

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1540 -->
# 四级单词 1540 · 不确定的
中文：不确定的
单词：###indefinite###
搭配：###an indefinite period### 一段不确定的时间
例句：How long will the library be closed? — For an ###indefinite### period; they're furnishing and equipping a new reading room. — How unfortunate!
译文：图书馆要关多久？——无限期；他们在布置、装备一间新阅览室。——真不巧！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1541 -->

中文：布置；提供
单词：###furnish###
搭配：###a furnished flat### 带家具的公寓
例句：How long will the library be closed? — For an indefinite period; they're ###furnishing### and equipping a new reading room. — How unfortunate!
译文：图书馆要关多久？——无限期；他们在布置、装备一间新阅览室。——真不巧！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1542 -->

中文：装备

搭配：###be equipped with ...### 配备有……
例句：How long will the library be closed? — For an indefinite period; they're furnishing and ###equipping### a new reading room. — How unfortunate!
译文：图书馆要关多久？——无限期；他们在布置、装备一间新阅览室。——真不巧！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1543 -->

中文：不幸的；不巧的
单词：###unfortunate###
搭配：###an unfortunate accident### 不幸的事故
例句：How long will the library be closed? — For an indefinite period; they're furnishing and equipping a new reading room. — How ###unfortunate###!
译文：图书馆要关多久？——无限期；他们在布置、装备一间新阅览室。——真不巧！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1544 -->
# 四级单词 1544 · 无疑地
中文：无疑地
单词：###doubtless###
搭配：###Doubtless, ...### 毫无疑问……
例句：###Doubtless###, false news circulates faster than the truth, and later explanations seldom catch up, so share news only after careful consideration.
译文：毫无疑问，假新闻比真相传得快，事后的解释很少能追上，所以分享新闻前要仔细考虑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1545 -->

单词：###circulate###
搭配：###circulate rumours### 散布谣言
例句：Doubtless, false news ###circulates### faster than the truth, and later explanations seldom catch up, so share news only after careful consideration.
译文：毫无疑问，假新闻比真相传得快，事后的解释很少能追上，所以分享新闻前要仔细考虑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1546 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1547 -->

搭配：###take ... into consideration### 把……考虑在内
例句：Doubtless, false news circulates faster than the truth, and later explanations seldom catch up, so share news only after careful ###consideration###.
译文：毫无疑问，假新闻比真相传得快，事后的解释很少能追上，所以分享新闻前要仔细考虑。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1548 -->
# 四级单词 1548 · 联系；交往
中文：联系；交往
单词：###associate###
搭配：###associate A with B### 把 A 与 B 联系起来
例句：We ###associate### the Silk Road with trade, but its continual exchange of ideas built a connection between peoples and helped civilize distant lands.
译文：我们把丝绸之路和贸易联系在一起，但它持续不断的思想交流也在各民族之间建立了联系，推动了远方土地的文明开化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1549 -->

中文：不断的
单词：###continual###
搭配：###continual changes### 不断的变化
例句：We associate the Silk Road with trade, but its ###continual### exchange of ideas built a connection between peoples and helped civilize distant lands.
译文：我们把丝绸之路和贸易联系在一起，但它持续不断的思想交流也在各民族之间建立了联系，推动了远方土地的文明开化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1550 -->

单词：###connection###
搭配：###in connection with ...### 与……有关
例句：We associate the Silk Road with trade, but its continual exchange of ideas built a ###connection### between peoples and helped civilize distant lands.
译文：我们把丝绸之路和贸易联系在一起，但它持续不断的思想交流也在各民族之间建立了联系，推动了远方土地的文明开化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1551 -->
# 四级单词 1551 · 使文明
中文：使文明
单词：###civilize###
搭配：###civilize a society### 使社会文明开化
例句：We associate the Silk Road with trade, but its continual exchange of ideas built a connection between peoples and helped ###civilize### distant lands.
译文：我们把丝绸之路和贸易联系在一起，但它持续不断的思想交流也在各民族之间建立了联系，推动了远方土地的文明开化。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1552 -->
# 四级单词 1552 · 下定义
中文：下定义
单词：###define###
搭配：###define a word### 给词下定义
例句：Success is hard to ###define### and not always measurable in money; for many, it means one thing, namely a satisfactory life.
译文：成功难以定义，也不总能用金钱衡量；对很多人来说，它只意味着一件事，即过上满意的生活。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1553 -->

单词：###measurable###
搭配：###measurable results### 可衡量的结果
例句：Success is hard to define and not always ###measurable### in money; for many, it means one thing, namely a satisfactory life.
译文：成功难以定义，也不总能用金钱衡量；对很多人来说，它只意味着一件事，即过上满意的生活。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1554 -->
# 四级单词 1554 · 即
中文：即
单词：###namely###
搭配：###two cities, namely A and B### 两座城市，即 A 和 B
例句：Success is hard to define and not always measurable in money; for many, it means one thing, ###namely### a satisfactory life.
译文：成功难以定义，也不总能用金钱衡量；对很多人来说，它只意味着一件事，即过上满意的生活。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1555 -->
# 四级单词 1555 · 令人满意的
中文：令人满意的
单词：###satisfactory###
搭配：###a satisfactory answer### 满意的答复
例句：Success is hard to define and not always measurable in money; for many, it means one thing, namely a ###satisfactory### life.
译文：成功难以定义，也不总能用金钱衡量；对很多人来说，它只意味着一件事，即过上满意的生活。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1556 -->
# 四级单词 1556 · 冷漠的
中文：冷漠的
单词：###indifferent###
搭配：###be indifferent to ...### 对……漠不关心
例句：Instead of being ###indifferent###, we should sympathize with those who mourn lost loved ones and offer them affection and comfort.
译文：我们不应冷漠，而应同情那些失去亲人、正在哀悼的人，给他们关爱和安慰。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1557 -->

例句：Instead of being indifferent, we should ###sympathize### with those who mourn lost loved ones and offer them affection and comfort.
译文：我们不应冷漠，而应同情那些失去亲人、正在哀悼的人，给他们关爱和安慰。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1558 -->
# 四级单词 1558 · 哀悼
中文：哀悼
单词：###mourn###
搭配：###mourn for sb.### 哀悼某人
例句：Instead of being indifferent, we should sympathize with those who ###mourn### lost loved ones and offer them affection and comfort.
译文：我们不应冷漠，而应同情那些失去亲人、正在哀悼的人，给他们关爱和安慰。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1559 -->
# 四级单词 1559 · 爱；感情
中文：爱；感情
单词：###affection###
搭配：###show affection### 表达爱意
例句：Instead of being indifferent, we should sympathize with those who mourn lost loved ones and offer them ###affection### and comfort.
译文：我们不应冷漠，而应同情那些失去亲人、正在哀悼的人，给他们关爱和安慰。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1560 -->
# 四级单词 1560 · 严重的；敏锐的
中文：严重的；敏锐的
单词：###acute###
搭配：###an acute shortage### 严重短缺
例句：The ###acute### water shortage, caused by irregular rain and an unstable climate, threatens farmers and city residents alike.
译文：由降雨不规律和气候不稳定造成的严重缺水，同样威胁着农民和城市居民。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1561 -->

# 四级单词 1605 · 幼稚的
中文：幼稚的
单词：###childish###
搭配：###childish behaviour### 幼稚的行为
例句：To an adult observer, what seems ###childish### may be acceptable, even wise, for a child trying to uncover how things work.
译文：在成年观察者看来幼稚的事，对一个想弄清事物如何运作的孩子来说，可能是可以接受的，甚至是明智的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1606 -->

# 四级单词 1608 · 可恨的
中文：可恨的
单词：###hateful###
搭配：###a hateful person### 可恶的人
例句：The ###hateful### king conquered the land through terror, held the reins of power, and threw his critics into jail.
译文：那个可恨的国王靠恐怖手段征服了这片土地，大权在握，把批评他的人都关进了监狱。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：The thief ###slit### the bag open with a naked blade without stabbing anyone; the dog's hair bristled, and its owner was struck dumb.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-cet4-words-w1635 -->
# 四级单词 1635 · 由于；预期的
中文：由于；预期的
单词：###due###
搭配：###due to ...### 由于……
例句：###Due### to its conjunction with the history course, the lecture covered the classification of political systems, including communism.
译文：由于和历史课相结合，这场讲座讲到了政治制度的分类，其中包括共产主义。
<!-- /leiji-note -->
